No la uses para interpretar renglón por renglón (dis-requerimientos). ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-analisis-spec))
- **dis-arquitectura-secuencia**: Diseñar la forma de la secuencia: grupos, cuándo usar una Sequence Call, Setup y Cleanup, qué monta y desmonta cada grupo, parámetros, manejo de error y estado seguro al abortar, y ejecución de N unidades. Úsala al pasar del plan a la estructura de la secuencia. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-arquitectura-secuencia))
- **dis-bom-accesos**: Aterrizar el BOM de accesos de la unidad: por dónde se toca cada señal —pin de conector, test point, o lectura por comando—, qué pines existen y no son NC, qué jumpers condicionan una señal, y qué señales no tienen acceso. Úsala al armar o revisar los accesos de la unidad. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-bom-accesos))
- **dis-bom-instrumentacion**: Aterrizar el BOM de instrumentación: qué capacidad pide cada medición y estímulo, qué instrumento del catálogo la cubre según su prioridad, cuántos canales y tarjetas hacen falta, y qué cambia con N unidades en serie o en paralelo. Úsala al armar o revisar el BOM de instrumentación. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-bom-instrumentacion))
- **dis-bom-puntos-cargas**: Aterrizar el BOM de puntos y cargas: tipo de contacto por punto, corriente por punto, cargas con valor, potencia y tolerancia, y cuándo hace falta conexión Kelvin. Úsala al armar o revisar la lista de puntos y cargas del fixture. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-bom-puntos-cargas))
- **dis-coll**: Explain DIS-COLL benefit for collaborators with math. Use when asked DIS-COLL, disoccupazione collaboratori Italy. ([terzastella/italy-work-skills](https://github.com/terzastella/italy-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-coll) · ★2)
- **dis-diagnostico**: Hacer que cada falla se pueda diagnosticar: nombres de paso y mensajes que dicen qué, dónde y qué revisar; datos para el técnico de reparación; y el efecto de parar al primer fallo. Úsala al nombrar pasos, redactar mensajes de falla o revisar un reporte. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-diagnostico))
- **dis-estrategia**: Diseñar la estrategia de prueba: qué se prueba en esta etapa, en qué orden, qué para al primer fallo, dónde se programa el firmware, y cómo se reparte el tiempo de ciclo. Úsala después del análisis del spec y antes de armar la secuencia. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-estrategia))
- **dis-kaynak-inceleme**: Kullanıcı indirdiği ya da gösterdiği bir dış depoyu, beceri koleksiyonunu, ikinci beyin veya ajan sistemini incelememizi, bizimkiyle karşılaştırmamızı ya da ondan bir şey almamızı istediğinde kullan. Ana sohbet (merkezî ajan) yükler. ([AlicanKaya192/Mindgate](https://github.com/AlicanKaya192/Mindgate/tree/HEAD/locales/tr/vault/_system/skills/external-source-review) · ★2)
- **dis-limites**: Decidir de dónde sale cada límite y cómo se escribe: del spec del cliente, derivado de una transferencia verificada, o de caracterización; familias de límite por tipo de interfaz; estados bajos en absoluto; fondo de escala del ADC; y Pending cuando no hay dato. Úsala al fijar o revisar límites. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-limites))
- **dis-medicion**: Hacer robusta cada medición: tiempo de asentamiento, rango fijo o automático, promedios, 2 o 4 hilos, referencia de tierra, carga para medir el estado apagado, reintentos y tiempos de espera. Úsala al diseñar un paso de medición o al investigar un paso inestable. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-medicion))
- **dis-plutot**: Pour chaque objection ratée dans un call de vente, donne au commercial la phrase exacte à dire la prochaine fois (« Dis plutôt »), plus la question de relance, en partant de ce que le prospect a vraiment dit et de ce que le commercial a répondu. Utilise ce skill dès que l'utilisateur demande quoi répondre, comment mieux gérer une objection, ce qu'il aurait dû dire, comment répondre à « c'est trop cher », « j'ai déjà essayé », « je dois en parler à… », ou veut s'entraîner sur les objections d'un call — par exemple « qu'est-ce que j'aurais dû répondre ? », « comment je gère son objection prix ?… ([weecloz/equipe-commerciale](https://github.com/weecloz/equipe-commerciale/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-plutot))
- **dis-requerimientos**: Segunda pasada del análisis: convertir cada renglón del spec (ya dentro del alcance autorizado) en un caso con sus cuatro respuestas —evidencia, límite, acceso, condición—, sacar de todo el spec el presupuesto de alimentación y las cargas, y levantar las preguntas que el spec no contesta. Úsala al interpretar requerimientos, al preguntar «qué pide este renglón», «qué falta en el spec» o «qué le preguntamos al cliente». No la uses para delimitar secciones (dis-analisis-spec) ni para escoger instrumentos (dis-bom-*). ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-requerimientos))
- **dis-resources**: Create and configure Altinn DIS platform custom resources as GitOps-ready Kubernetes manifests — PostgreSQL Database and DatabaseServer, Azure Key Vault, and ApplicationIdentity. Use this whenever the user wants to provision or wire up DIS infrastructure for an Altinn app: creating a database, standing up a postgres server (dedicated or shared), requesting a key vault or secret store, or creating an application / managed identity — even when they don't name the CRD or the operator. Do NOT use this for developing the dis-* operators themselves (controller/reconciler code, regenerating CRD mani… ([Altinn/altinn-platform](https://github.com/Altinn/altinn-platform/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dis-resources) · ★13)
- **dis-seguridad**: Proteger a la unidad, a la estación y al operador: orden de energizado, límites de corriente, descarga de capacitores y baterías, alto voltaje e interlocks, conexión y desconexión, y estado seguro al abortar. Úsala en toda secuencia que energice la unidad y al revisar una antes de liberarla. ([b101systems/agent-skills](https://github.com/b101systems/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dis-seguridad))
- **dis-tarama**: Depo dışına bakma ve bulunanı bu projenin para biriminde fiyatlandırma yordamı. Yeni bir eksen/fikir/iyileştirme açılmadan önce, "bu hedefe ulaşmanın başka yolu var mı", "bunu dışarıda çözen biri var mı", "şu makale/proje/araç işimize yarar mı" sorulduğunda, ya da bir dış kaynak (makale, GitHub projesi, ürün, kişi) değerlendirilecekse kullan. Tetikleyiciler: dış tarama, literatür, makale, başka kim yapmış, dışarıda var mı, bu fikir işe yarar mı, yeni yaklaşım, vizyonu genişlet. - **dis-ticaret-mevzuati**: İthalat-ihracat rejimi, ithal lisansları, antidamping ve korunma önlemleri, ek mali yükümlülük ve gözetim uygulamaları söz konusu olduğunda; dış ticaret düzenlemeleri ile gümrük işlemini birlikte değerlendirmek için kullanılır. - **dis-tipos-de-interfaz**: Clasificar cada señal de la unidad en un tipo de interfaz —entrada analógica, entrada digital, salida de potencia, riel, referencia, bus, etc.— y aplicar el patrón de prueba de su tipo: cuántos estados, qué estímulo, qué familia de límite. - **disc21-eval**: This benchmark evaluates image copy detection systems under realistic, adversarial conditions. It probes a model's ability to match transformed query images against a large reference database while resisting geometric, color, overlay, and deepfake manipulations. The setup emphasizes scalability and robustness in a high-false-positive-rate, needle-in-haystack search regime. Use when the user wants to benchmark on DISC21, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports micro Average Precision. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/disc21-eval) · ★3)
- **discanvis-analysis**: Answer biological questions from DisCanVisFlow output that already exists — cross-referencing disorder, motifs, PTMs, domains, structure and variants to reach a conclusion rather than producing files. Use this skill when someone asks what the data means: "which region of TP53 is most mutated", "are the ClinVar variants in disordered regions", "what motifs sit in the IDRs of CTNNB1", "is this protein a phase separator", "do the PTM sites overlap the binding regions", "compare disorder between these two isoforms", "which of my 200 genes have long IDRs", or any request to summarise, rank, plot o… ([Nosyfire/DisCanVisFlow](https://github.com/Nosyfire/DisCanVisFlow/tree/HEAD/skills/discanvis-analysis))
- **discanvis-new-track**: Add a new annotation track to the DisCanVisFlow pipeline end to end — worker, tests, Nextflow process, wiring, config parameters, and every place the track has to be registered so it is discoverable and documented. Use this skill whenever someone wants to extend the pipeline with a new data source or predictor: "add a track for X", "integrate this database", "wire up a new predictor", "we should pull in FuzDrop", "add DUST/TRF complexity", "can we include this new LLPS dataset", or when reviewing whether an existing track was fully integrated. The value is the registration checklist: a track… ([Nosyfire/DisCanVisFlow](https://github.com/Nosyfire/DisCanVisFlow/tree/HEAD/skills/discanvis-new-track))
- **discanvis-troubleshoot**: Diagnose DisCanVisFlow runs that failed, hung, or produced empty output. Use this skill when a pipeline run goes wrong in any way: "the run crashed", "nextflow can't find java", "my output TSV is empty / header only", "the process was killed", "it says exit status 137", "conservation is empty", "IUPred produced nothing", "the download is 0 bytes", "it's been running for hours", "-resume isn't picking up my fix", "OutOfMemoryError", "storeDir file is corrupt", or when someone pastes a Nextflow error trace. Empty output is the important case — the pipeline is built to degrade rather than crash,… ([Nosyfire/DisCanVisFlow](https://github.com/Nosyfire/DisCanVisFlow/tree/HEAD/skills/discanvis-troubleshoot))

- **discard**: 現在のセッション履歴を要約・蒸留し、次のセッション用の再開プロンプト（ブリッジプロンプト）を生成するスキル。/discard で使用。 ([Rikemen/math-by-making](https://github.com/Rikemen/math-by-making/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/discard))
- **discard-generated-assets**: Revert the 9 generated PNG/icon files under public/ that scripts/build-assets.mjs regenerates non-deterministically (ImageMagick `convert` embeds fresh metadata/timestamps even when the visual output is identical). Run this after `pnpm build`, `pnpm postinstall`, `pnpm test:e2e`, or any command that shells out to build-assets.mjs, whenever `git status` shows those files as modified but no icon/logo source actually changed. Trigger phrases: "limpia los iconos regenerados", "descarta los PNG de test", "revert generated assets". ([vladimir1284/lamula-webviewer](https://github.com/vladimir1284/lamula-webviewer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/discard-generated-assets))
- **discard-whitespace-hunks**: Discard whitespace-only diff hunks from the git working tree while preserving real content changes. Use this skill whenever the user asks to clean up whitespace noise in a git diff, discard formatting-only changes, remove whitespace/indentation/line-break-only hunks, strip cosmetic diff noise, or keep only meaningful changes in unstaged modifications. Also use it when the user says things like "my diff is full of whitespace junk" or "I only want the real changes" or "drop the reformatting hunks". ([akaihola/skills-akaihola](https://github.com/akaihola/skills-akaihola/tree/HEAD/discard-whitespace-hunks) · ★2)
- **discern**: Explore the paths first where none are on record (the `invent` skill), then attack every path adversarially with one blind attacker per path, commit to one, and present the commitment — consequences for the user to confirm, kills on the record. Use when the user asks to run discernment on a tracked piece of work (`/discern`, or in words), or asks for a ready-made plan to be attacked before it is built. A work whose `next:` names this stage is not that ask, because the flow never advances itself, and a plan handed over to be implemented is not an invitation to attack it or to start the flow. ([donald-ada/workinggenius](https://github.com/donald-ada/workinggenius/tree/HEAD/skills/discern) · ★6)
- **discern**: The Companion path's discernment loop — clarity for a decision or a crossroads, Socratic in spirit. Surfaces untested assumptions, contingent beliefs ("what has to be true for this to make sense?"), and blind spots (who or what isn't in the room?). Checks options against the person's living values, and notices when a value itself is up for revision. Doesn't decide — clarifies so the person can. Ends by bridging to the felt sense of the choice. Triggers: /discern, "help me discern", "I have a decision", "I'm at a crossroads", "what's true here", "help me see clearly", "should I". ([opencosmos-ai/opencosmos](https://github.com/opencosmos-ai/opencosmos/tree/HEAD/public/inception/paths/companion/loops/discern) · ★5)
- **discern**: Product Discernment — run a 4ds spec's Product Discernment & Diligence checklist against a code change — a task's diff, a branch (e.g. from a Coding-type /4ds:delegate worktree run), specific files, or the current uncommitted diff. Works on code you wrote yourself too, not just Claude's output. Distinct from Process Discernment (process-reviewer, runs automatically after coder/writer) and Performance Discernment (delegate's check-in). Explicit-invocation only. ([febradc-github/4ds](https://github.com/febradc-github/4ds/tree/HEAD/skills/discern) · ★1)
- **discern-agent-voice**: Write or review communication whose primary reader is a coding agent. Use for MCP descriptions, JSON instructions, setup briefs, skills, hints, `llms.txt`, machine-oriented documentation, and the public For Agents page. Declare operational or public mode before drafting. ([discern-sh/discern](https://github.com/discern-sh/discern/tree/HEAD/project/skills/discern-agent-voice) · ★3)
- **discern-await-the-fleet**: Wait for another workstream with one blocking `discern_await` call — a sibling branch green, its work landed, or the trunk moved — then compose what arrived. Use when a task depends on another agent's in-flight work; when the user says "wait for", "watch", or "check back on" a branch, worktree, sibling agent, or the trunk; when a brief names a dependency to await; or when tempted to poll status, sleep, or ask the user to relay readiness. Not for sub-agents inside your own session. Bundled with discern. ([discern-sh/discern](https://github.com/discern-sh/discern/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/discern-await-the-fleet) · ★3)
- **discern-brand-voice**: Write or review discern's public-facing brand copy: website pages, campaigns, launch material, social posts, founder essays, product marketing, and public explanations. Use this skill when the reader is deciding whether discern belongs in their future. Do not use it for CLI messages, exact documentation, MCP instructions, or operational agent instructions. ([discern-sh/discern](https://github.com/discern-sh/discern/tree/HEAD/project/skills/discern-brand-voice) · ★3)
- **discern-clear-the-decks**: Clear the decks — sweep out the clutter agent-built codebases accumulate, the stack-agnostic agentic smells — duplicated helpers, dead code from abandoned approaches, one-caller indirection, leftover scaffolding, convention drift. Every cut proven safe, landed as small behavior-preserving commits, with the entropy capped by a standard so the mess can only shrink. Use when asked to clean up, tidy, simplify, or de-slop a codebase, to remove dead code or duplication, when a project "is getting messy" after many agent sessions, or as periodic maintenance between features. Bundled with discern. ([discern-sh/discern](https://github.com/discern-sh/discern/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/discern-clear-the-decks) · ★3)
- **discern-cure-a-bug**: Cure a bug — fix every instance of the underlying defect and leave a permanent guard so it can never return; patching only the instance you were shown is symptomatic relief. Carries the whole discipline in three procedures — diagnose first when the cause is unproven (reproduce the failure, falsify hypotheses, prove the cause instead of patching a guess), then cure the class, and audit existing tests and guards for coverage that guards less than it appears to. Use when fixing any bug or asked to fix something "properly", "at the root cause", or "for good"; when investigating why something is h… ([discern-sh/discern](https://github.com/discern-sh/discern/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/discern-cure-a-bug) · ★3)
- **discern-delegate-work**: Turn the work you've been discussing into complete, self-contained prompts for fresh agents in their own worktrees — one handoff, a parallel fan-out, or staged briefs — then review what lands adversarially. Use when the user wants to delegate, hand off, or spin off work to a new, separate, or clean agent session, wants to parallelise a large effort, split work across agents or worktrees, asks you to "write a prompt for another agent", or to "kick this off in a fresh worktree".
